- **Bank of Korea governor backs CBDCs, deposit tokens in first address** [(원문)](https://cointelegraph.com/news/new-bank-of-korea-governor-backs-cbdcs-deposit-tokens-first-address?utm_source=rss_feed&utm_medium=rss&utm_campaign=rss_partner_inbound)
  한국은행 신임 총재 신현송이 CBDCs와 deposit tokens에 적극 지지 표명. 스테이블코인 미언급으로 정부의 선택적 디지털 자산 정책 방향 시사. 한국의 CBDC 및 토큰화 자산 추진 의지 재확인.
- **European banks tap Fireblocks for MiCA-compliant euro stablecoin** [(원문)](https://cointelegraph.com/news/european-banks-tap-fireblocks-for-mica-compliant-euro-stablecoin?utm_source=rss_feed&utm_medium=rss&utm_campaign=rss_partner_inbound)
  유럽 12개 주요 은행이 Fireblocks와 협력해 MiCA 준수 유로 스테이블코인 개발 추진, 2026년 하반기 출시 예정. 개념 단계의 규제 준수가 구체적 구현으로 진화. 전통금융의 블록체인 통합 가속화 신호.
- **Inside the 'fake police raid' that forced a $1M Bitcoin transfer** [(원문)](https://cointelegraph.com/news/crypto-coercion-attack-fake-police-raid?utm_source=rss_feed&utm_medium=rss&utm_campaign=rss_partner_inbound)
  위장 경찰 신분을 악용한 강압 사건으로 $1M 규모 비트코인 탈취 발생. 기존 디지털 해킹 중심 위협에서 물리적 강압(wrench attack)으로의 전환을 보여주는 사례. 암호자산 보안 위협의 범위 확대.
- **Singapore’s OCBC launches tokenized gold fund on Ethereum and Solana** [(원문)](https://cointelegraph.com/news/ocbc-tokenized-gold-fund-ethereum-solana?utm_source=rss_feed&utm_medium=rss&utm_campaign=rss_partner_inbound)

- **Arbitrum freezes $71M of Ether connected to Kelp exploit** [(원문)](https://cointelegraph.com/news/arbitrum-freezes-71m-ether-connected-kelp-exploit?utm_source=rss_feed&utm_medium=rss&utm_campaign=rss_partner_inbound)

- **Code is ‘functional’ free speech under the First Amendment: Coin Center** [(원문)](https://cointelegraph.com/news/crypto-code-free-speech-coin-center-first-amendment?utm_source=rss_feed&utm_medium=rss&utm_campaign=rss_partner_inbound)

- **Coinbase Out, Anchorage In: Grayscale Reworks Its Hyperliquid ETF Custody - Yellow.com** [(원문)](https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMicEFVX3lxTFBrNjVPOC1JU2pwakt6eTJwUEd6OF81Sk5hdUp2M2dsUGM3d2JXa0p4bHRsUF96d19SNGN5SG5rNld2Ml9kZEZiOHozMmRxdFZBdjhsb3ZnWWlkV1BDaGt4Z0RUSU1jbEhKM1FkR0xEeWk?oc=5)

- **Aave’s core markets hit 100% utilization at once, and that's not a good thing** [(원문)](https://www.coindesk.com/business/2026/04/21/aave-s-core-markets-hit-100-utilization-at-once-here-is-what-it-means-and-it-s-not-good)

- **White House Crypto Adviser Hints at ‘Breakthrough’ Bitcoin Reserve Move** [(원문)](https://decrypt.co/365801/white-house-crypto-adviser-hints-at-breakthrough-bitcoin-reserve-move)
